## 📝 Duplicate Page Titles

Pages should have a descriptive, unique `<title>` so users can tell them apart in browser tabs, history, and bookmarks ([WCAG 2.4.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/page-titled.html)).

- **"We apologize for the inconvenience..."** — 3 pages:
  - https://www.gsa.gov/sandbox/offline-pages/pages-that-are-offline-and-can-be-deleted-after-aug-10-2026/offline-about-us-content/featured-roles

## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. ## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 4 page(s) - 4 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [heading-order](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/heading-order?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate
**Issue**: Heading levels should only increase by one

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<h4>
          Other GSA Blogs
    </h4>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
h4
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('h4')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://gsa.gov/blog/2023/11/28/gsa-smartpay-which-supports-federal-purchases-and-reinvests-in-mission-celebrates-25-years
- https://gsa.gov/blog/2021/07/20/accessibility-starts-with-us
- https://gsa.gov/blog/2023/10/25/promoting-accessibility-and-inclusion-during-national-disability-employment-awareness-month
- https://gsa.gov/blog/2023/12/15/no-two-screens-look-the-same-sharpening-digital-accessibility-through-inclusivity

---

> 💡 **Pro Tip**: These patterns likely come from shared components or templates in your codebase. Fix them in the component/template source, and the fix will propagate to all affected pages.

### https://gsa.gov/real-estate/child-care-services/find-a-child-care-center-near-you

#### Rule: [aria-valid-attr](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/aria-valid-attr?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**WCAG**: [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- ⚠️ **Corroborated by AccessLint** — flagged on the same element by 2 independent tools, a stronger signal that this is a real issue rather than a false positive.
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid names
- HTML: `<a class="paginate_button previous disabled" aria-controls="child-care-centers-table" aria-disabled="true" aria-role="link" data-dt-idx="previous" tabindex="-1" id="child-care-centers-table_previous">Previous</a>`
- XPath: `#child-care-centers-table_previous`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid names
- HTML: `<a class="paginate_button current" aria-controls="child-care-centers-table" aria-role="link" aria-current="page" data-dt-idx="0" tabindex="0">1</a>`
- XPath: `.current`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid names
- HTML: `<a class="paginate_button " aria-controls="child-care-centers-table" aria-role="link" data-dt-idx="1" tabindex="0">2</a>`
- XPath: `a[data-dt-idx="1"]`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid names
- HTML: `<a class="paginate_button " aria-controls="child-care-centers-table" aria-role="link" data-dt-idx="2" tabindex="0">3</a>`
- XPath: `a[data-dt-idx="2"]`

**Failure 5:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid names
- HTML: `<a class="paginate_button " aria-controls="child-care-centers-table" aria-role="link" data-dt-idx="3" tabindex="0">4</a>`
- XPath: `a[data-dt-idx="3"]`

*... and 3 more failures for this rule*

### https://gsa.gov/buy-through-us/products-and-services/transportation-and-logistics-services/fleet-management/vehicle-leasing/find-a-fleet-service-representative

#### Rule: [aria-valid-attr-value](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/aria-valid-attr-value?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**WCAG**: [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid values
- HTML: `<input type="search" class="" placeholder="Filter results" aria-controls="Fleet Service Representatives">`
- XPath: `input[placeholder="Filter results"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid values
- HTML: `<th scope="col" class="sorting" aria-controls="Fleet Service Representatives" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="width: 225.031px; padding-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 0px; border-top-width: 0px; border-bottom-width: 0px; height: 0px;">`
- XPath: `#Fleet\ Service\ Representatives > thead > tr > th:nth-child(1)`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid values
- HTML: `<th scope="col" class="sorting" aria-controls="Fleet Service Representatives" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="width: 523.953px; padding-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 0px; border-top-width: 0px; border-bottom-width: 0px; height: 0px;">`
- XPath: `#Fleet\ Service\ Representatives > thead > tr > th:nth-child(2)`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: ARIA attributes must conform to valid values
- HTML: `<th scope="col" class="sorting" aria-controls="Fleet Service Representatives" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="width: 178.016px; padding-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 0px; border-top-width: 0px; border-bottom-width: 0px; height: 0px;">`
- XPath: `#Fleet\ Service\ Representatives > thead > tr > th:nth-child(3)`

### https://gsa.gov/

#### Rule: [image-redundant-alt](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/image-redundant-alt?application=playwright)
**Impact**: minor

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: Alternative text of images should not be repeated as text
- HTML: `<img loading="lazy" src="/system/files/768x428-5-USEITActandOccupancyData.jpg" height="428" alt="USE IT ACT AND OCCUPANCY DATA" typeof="foaf:Image" class="width-full">`
- XPath: `img[alt="USE IT ACT AND OCCUPANCY DATA"]`

#### Rule: [landmark-main-is-top-level](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/landmark-main-is-top-level?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- ⚠️ **Corroborated by AccessLint** — flagged on the same element by 2 independent tools, a stronger signal that this is a real issue rather than a false positive.
- Message: Main landmark should not be contained in another landmark
- HTML: `<div class="container" id="page-content" role="main" tabindex="-1">`
- XPath: `#page-content`

#### Rule: [landmark-no-duplicate-main](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/landmark-no-duplicate-main?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: Document should not have more than one main landmark
- HTML: `<main class="usa-section" id="main-content">`
- XPath: `#main-content`

#### Rule: [landmark-unique](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/landmark-unique?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: Landmarks should have a unique role or role/label/title (i.e. accessible name) combination
- HTML: `<nav class="tbm tbm-main tbm-arrows" id="tbm-main" data-breakpoint="1200" data-initialized="true">`
- XPath: `#tbm-main`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: Landmarks should have a unique role or role/label/title (i.e. accessible name) combination
- HTML: `<main class="usa-section" id="main-content">`
- XPath: `#main-content`

### https://gsa.gov/real-estate/find-a-historic-federal-building/agriculture-south-building-washington-dc

#### Rule: [definition-list](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/definition-list?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**WCAG**: [SC 1.3.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/info-and-relationships) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-27
- Message: <dl> elements must only directly contain properly-ordered <dt> and <dd> groups, <script>, <template> or <div> elements
- HTML: `<dl><dd><p>&nbsp;</p></dd></dl>`
- XPath: `dl`
